y below zero means motion in the negative direction."],["Add them for displacement","+6+(−6)=0 m.","The object returns to its starting position."],["Add magnitudes for distance","6+6=12 m.","Integrating velocity gives displacement. For total distance, accumulate speed instead."]],"notice":"The area under a speed graph gives distance. Signed area under a velocity graph gives displacement."},"rescue":"Treat each small time interval as a receipt for movement. A negative velocity produces a negative displacement receipt. Add those signed receipts for the change in position."},"calculus-5":{"needs":"Differentiate a quadratic and solve a simple equation. You can read this lesson after the derivative introduction.","prerequisites":["calculus-2"],"terms":[["Constraint","A condition that limits the allowed choices, such as available fence."],["Stationary point","A point where the derivative is zero."],["Endpoint","A boundary of the allowed input range."]],"second":{"title":"When the stationary point is outside the allowed range","problem":"Revenue contribution is f(x)=−x²+8x, but a constraint allows only 0≤x≤3. Can I pay by card?’","steps":[["Start with what you want","Quiero comer.","Quiero means ‘I want.’ The next verb, comer, names what you want to do, so it stays in the infinitive. We don’t change both verbs."],["Say that you’re hungry","Tengo hambre.","Spanish uses tener here: literally, ‘I have hunger.’ Estoy hambre would copy the English structure, but it isn’t the Spanish expression."],["Ask about paying","¿Puedo pagar con tarjeta?","Puedo means ‘I can’; pagar is ‘to pay.’ Raising it as a question asks whether you can do it. Tengo hambre. ¿Puedo pagar con tarjeta?","Notice that the three opening words—quiero, tengo, puedo—already tell the listener that you’re talking about yourself. You don’t need to add yo to every sentence."]],"check":"Keep the verb pattern and change the detail: Quiero beber agua. Puedo pagar en efectivo. const play=async sequence=>{
 stop();const run=v105AudioRun,status=$('v105PianoStatus'),Context=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext,beat=60/Number($('v107Tempo').value);
 if(!Context){status.textContent='Audio isn’t available here. Follow the written notes below.';return}
 try{
  v105Audio=v105Audio||new Context();await v105Audio.resume();
  for(let i=0;i<sequence.length;i++){
   if(run!==v105AudioRun||!host.isConnected)return;
   const beats=id==='piano-2'&&sequence.length===7&&i===6?2:1,duration=beat*beats,note=sequence[i];
   host.querySelectorAll('[data-demo-note]').forEach(e=>e.classList.toggle('playing',e.dataset.demoNote===note));
   status.textContent='Playing '+note+' · note '+(i+1)+' of '+sequence.length+' · '+beats+(beats===1?' beat':' beats (hold)');
   const semitones={C:0,D:2,E:4,F:5,G:7,A:9,B:11},midi=12*(Number(note[1])+1)+semitones[note[0]],osc=v105Audio.createOscillator(),gain=v105Audio.createGain(),now=v105Audio.currentTime;
   osc.frequency.value=440*2**((midi-69)/12);gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0,now);gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(.09,now+.02);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+duration*.94);osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(v105Audio.destination);activeOscillators.add(osc);osc.onended=()=>{activeOscillators.delete(osc);osc.disconnect();gain.disconnect()};osc.start(now);osc.stop(now+duration*.97);
   await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,duration*1000));
  }
  if(run===v105AudioRun){stop();status.textContent='Finished: '+sequence.join(' – ')+(id==='piano-2'&&sequence.length===7?' · final E held for two beats.':'')}
 }catch{stop();status.textContent='The sound couldn’t play. You can still follow the written notes.'}
};

}

function keyboard(){return `<div class="v97-keyboard" aria-label="Playable note names: C through B"><button data-note="C4">C<small>C</small></button><button class="v97-black" data-note="C#4">C♯</button><button data-note="D4">D<small>D</small></button><button class="v97-black" data-note="D#4">D♯</button><button data-note="E4">E<small>E</small></button><button data-note="F4">F<small>F</small></button><button class="v97-black" data-note="F#4">F♯</button><button data-note="G4">G<small>G</small></button><button class="v97-black" data-note="G#4">G♯</button><button data-note="A4">A<small>A</small></button><button class="v97-black" data-note="A#4">A♯</button><button data-note="B4">B<small>B</small></button><button data-note="C5">C<small>C</small></button></div><p id="v97Note" class="v97-note" aria-live="polite">Tap a key to hear it. The white key left of the first pair of black keys is C.</p>`}
function play(note){const semitone={'C':0,'C#':1,'D':2,'D#':3,'E':4,'F':5,'F#':6,'G':7,'G#':8,'A':9,'A#':10,'B':11};const m=/^([A-G]#?)([45])$/.exec(note);if(!m)return;const midi=12*(+m[2]+1)+semitone[m[1]];const hz=440*Math.pow(2,(midi-69)/12);$('v97Note').textContent='You played '+note.replace('#','♯')+'. Now try C–D–E, slowly.';try{const Context=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!Context)return;if(!audio)audio=new Context();audio.resume();const o=audio.createOscillator(),g=audio.createGain(),now=audio.currentTime;o.type='sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(hz,now);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.12,now+.02);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+.55);o.connect(g);g.connect(audio.destination);o.start(now);o.stop(now+.6)}catch{}}
function renderQuestion(){const L=current,q=L.questions[state.question],root=$('v97Quiz');if(!q){root.innerHTML='';return}
root.innerHTML=`<section class="v97-quiz"><small>YOUR TURN · ${state.question+1} / ${L.questions.length}</small><h3>${escape(q.prompt)}</h3><label for="v102Attempt">Try without options first</label><textarea id="v102Attempt" rows="3" placeholder="Write an answer and the reason or calculation."></textarea><div class="v102-actions"><button id="v102Options">Compare with the options</button><button class="v102-secondary" id="v102OptionsHelp">I’m stuck — show options</button><button class="v102-secondary" id="v102Memory">Hide explanation while I try</button></div><p id="v102ChoiceStatus" role="status"></p><div class="v97-choices" hidden>${q.choices.map((choice,i)=>({choice,i,r:Math.random()})).sort((a,b)=>a.r-b.r).map(({choice,i})=>`<button type="button" class="v97-choice" data-choice="${i}">${escape(choice)}</button>`).join('')}</div><div id="v97Feedback" class="v97-feedback" role="status" hidden></div><div class="v97-actions"><button id="v97Clue" class="v97-light" type="button">Give me a clue</button><button id="v97Reveal" class="v97-light" type="button">Show the answer and why</button></div><p class="v97-status" id="v97Status">${read()[L.id]?.clean?'You have answered this lesson independently before. Try again if you like.':'The hints and worked answer are here if you need them.'}</p></section>`;
root.querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>choose(Number(b.dataset.choice)));
const showOptions=assisted=>{if(!assisted&&!$('v102Attempt').value.trim()){$('v102ChoiceStatus').textContent='Try one sentence first, or choose “I’m stuck.”';$('v102Attempt').focus();return}state.helped ||= assisted;root.querySelector('.v97-choices').hidden=false;$('v102Options').disabled=true;$('v102OptionsHelp').disabled=true;};$('v102Options').onclick=()=>showOptions(false);$('v102OptionsHelp').onclick=()=>showOptions(true);$('v102Memory').onclick=()=>{for(const sel of ['#v101How','#v101Explain','.v97-scene']){const e=$('v97Main').querySelector(sel);if(e)e.hidden=true}$('v102ChoiceStatus').textContent='Explanation hidden. Use the lesson links to reopen it whenever you need.'};
$('v97Clue').onclick=()=>{state.helped=true;state.clues=(state.clues||0)+1;display('<strong>A clue:</strong> '+escape(state.clues===1?q.nudge:q.hint),'help')};$('v97Reveal').onclick=()=>{state.helped=true;save(L.id,'reveals');const right=q.correct;complete(`<strong>Answer: ${escape(q.choices[right])}.</strong> ${escape(q.feedback[right])}<p>${escape(L.why)}</p>`,'help')};}
function display(html,tone='help'){let el=$('v97Feedback');el.hidden=false;el.dataset.tone=tone;el.innerHTML=html;}
function choose(i){if(state.complete)return;const q=current.questions[state.question],right=q.correct,b=$(`v97Quiz`)?.querySelector(`[data-choice="${i}"]`);if(i!==right){state.missed=true;save(current.id,'tries');b?.classList.add('v97-wrong');display(`${escape(q.feedback[i])}`,'wrong');return}
const clean=!state.missed&&!state.helped;save(current.id,clean?'clean':'guided');complete(`<strong>${escape(q.choices[i])}</strong><p>${escape(q.feedback[i])}</p>`,'correct');b?.classList.add('v97-correct');}
function complete(html,tone){state.complete=true;display(html,tone);$('v97Quiz').querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);$('v97Clue').disabled=true;$('v97Reveal').disabled=true;const a=document.createElement('button');a.type='button';a.textContent=state.question===0?'Try the new example':'Try a different situation';a.onclick=()=>{if(state.question===0){state.question=1;state.missed=false;state.helped=false;state.complete=false;state.clues=0;renderQuestion()}else {v102ShowTransfer();a.disabled=true}};$('v97Quiz').querySelector('.v97-actions').append(a)}
function answerQuestion(text){const ask=String(text||'').trim();if(!ask)return;const lower=ask.toLowerCase();let msg='';const specific=current.faq.find(x=>x[0].toLowerCase()===lower)||current.faq.find(x=>{const tok=x[0].toLowerCase().split(/\W+/).filter(t=>t.length>4);return tok.length>=2&&tok.some(t=>lower.includes(t))});if(specific)msg=specific[1];else if(/why|how come|reason|intuition|work|explain/.test(lower))msg=current.why+' '+current.rule;else if(/step|work(ing|ed)? example|solve|start|how do/.test(lower))msg=current.steps.map((v,i)=>(i+1)+'. '+v).join('\n');else if(/mistake|confus|wrong|error|trap|instead/.test(lower))msg=current.trap;else if(/formula|rule|remember|equation|definition/.test(lower))msg=current.rule;else if(/practice|question|test|try/.test(lower))msg='Try the two questions above. The second changes the situation, so you can tell whether you really understand the method.';else msg='I can explain the steps, why the method works, or a common mistake in this lesson. Try “show the steps” or “why does this work?” For questions outside this lesson, open the full course.';
const root=$('v97Answers'),item=document.createElement('div');item.className='v97-response';const q=document.createElement('strong');q.textContent='You asked: '+ask;const p=document.createElement('p');p.textContent=msg;item.append(q,p);root.prepend(item);$('v97AskInput').value='';}
function mountHome(){const hero=$('v90Hero');if(!hero||hero.querySelector('#v97HomeStart'))return;const n=document.createElement('div');n.id='v97HomeStart';n.className='v97-start';n.innerHTML='<div><strong>Lessons and practice</strong><p>Read an example, try the questions, then work through another problem. Your original full courses are still here.</p></div><button type="button" id="v97Quick">Teach me the first step</button>';hero.append(n);$('v97Quick').onclick=()=>{let fav='spanish';try{const p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('studytable_v90_preferences')||'{}');if(NAME[p.lastSubject])fav=p.lastSubject}catch{}open(fav)};}
// Keep the substantial in-file V23/V27/V71/V89 courses. Do not advertise archive links that require missing separate HTML files.

}
const originalEnhance=window.studyTableV90?.enhanceHome; if(originalEnhance){window.studyTableV90.enhanceHome=function(){originalEnhance();mountHome()}}

const KEY='studytable:v100:recall',LEGACY='studytable_v98_recall',THEME='studytable:v100:theme';
const DAY=86400000,el=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const escapeHTML=v=>String(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const validObject=v=>v!==null&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v);
let deck=[],index=0,helped=false,sessionAttempts=0,repeatCount={},selected='all';
function load(){
 try{
  const stored=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'null');
  if(validObject(stored)&&validObject(stored.cards))return stored;
  const legacy=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LEGACY)||'null');
  if(validObject(legacy)){const next={schema:1,cards:legacy};localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(next));return next}
 }catch(e){console.warn('Recall progress unavailable',e)}
 return {schema:1,cards:{}};
}
function save(state){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state))}catch(e){console.warn('Could not save recall progress',e)}}
function due(card,state){const p=state.cards[card.id];return !p||!Number.isFinite(p.next)||p.next<=Date.now()}
function shuffle(items){const a=items.slice();for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]]}return a}
function subjectOptions(){const counts={};for(const c of CARDS)counts[c.subject]=(counts[c.subject]||0)+1;return counts}
function start(subject='all'){
 selected=subject;const state=load(),pool=CARDS.filter(c=>subject==='all'||c.subject===subject);
 deck=shuffle(pool.filter(c=>due(c,state)));
 if(!deck.length)deck=shuffle(pool).slice(0,Math.min(6,pool.length));
 index=0;helped=false;sessionAttempts=0;repeatCount={};
 const d=el('v98Dialog');if(!d.open)d.showModal();render();
}
function menu(){
 const state=load(),counts=subjectOptions();
 el('v98Body').innerHTML=`<div class="v100-meta">YOUR PRACTICE ROOM</div><h2>Make it stick.</h2><p>Try a question before looking at the explanation. Choose a subject or review everything. You can always ask for a hint.</p><div class="v100-subjects"><button type="button" data-subject="all">All subjects<span>${CARDS.filter(c=>due(c,state)).length} due · ${CARDS.length} total</span></button>${Object.entries(counts).map(([k,v])=>`<button type="button" data-subject="${escapeHTML(k)}">${escapeHTML(k[0].toUpperCase()+k.slice(1))}<span>${CARDS.filter(c=>c.subject===k&&due(c,state)).length} due · ${v} total</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v100-meta">Answers are self-checked. This version does not pretend to grade free-form reasoning automatically.</p>`;
 el('v98Body').querySelectorAll('[data-subject]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>start(b.dataset.subject));
}
function render(){
 const c=deck[index];
 if(!c){
  const state=load(),remaining=CARDS.filter(x=>due(x,state)).length;
  el('v98Body').innerHTML=`<div class="v100-meta">SESSION COMPLETE</div><h2>Practice finished</h2><p>You worked through ${sessionAttempts} question attempt${sessionAttempts===1?'':'s'}. ${remaining} question${remaining===1?' is':'s are'} currently ready for review across the full course.</p><div class="v98-actions"><button id="v100Again">Choose another subject</button><button class="v100-secondary" id="v100Done">Return to learning</button></div>`;
  el('v100Again').onclick=menu;el('v100Done').onclick=()=>el('v98Dialog').close();return;
 }
 helped=false;
 el('v98Body').innerHTML=`<div class="v100-meta">${escapeHTML(c.subject.toUpperCase())} · QUESTION ${index+1} OF ${deck.length} · ${escapeHTML(c.lesson)}</div><h2>${escapeHTML(c.prompt)}</h2><p>Work it out in your own words. Include the steps you tried.</p><label for="v98Response">Your answer or reasoning</label><textarea id="v98Response" rows="4" placeholder="Write what you think, or explain your steps."></textarea><div class="v98-actions"><button class="v100-secondary" id="v98Hint" type="button">Give me a hint</button><button id="v98Reveal" type="button">Check my answer</button><button class="v100-secondary" id="v98Stuck" type="button">I'm stuck</button></div><p id="v98Help" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><section id="v98Solution" class="v100-explanation" hidden></section>`;
 el('v98Hint').onclick=()=>{helped=true;el('v98Help').textContent=c.hint};
 el('v98Reveal').onclick=()=>reveal(c,false);
 el('v98Stuck').onclick=()=>reveal(c,true);
 el('v98Response').focus();
}
function reveal(c,stuck){
 const attempt=el('v98Response').value.trim();
 if(!stuck&&!attempt){el('v98Help').textContent='Write an attempt first, or choose “I’m stuck” to learn the solution.';el('v98Response').focus();return}
 const independent=!!attempt&&!helped&&!stuck;
 el('v98Reveal').disabled=true;el('v98Stuck').disabled=true;el('v98Hint').disabled=true;el('v98Response').readOnly=true;
 const box=el('v98Solution');box.hidden=false;
 box.innerHTML=`<h3>Worked answer</h3><p><strong>${escapeHTML(c.answer)}</strong></p><p>${escapeHTML(c.why)}</p>${attempt?`<h3>Your attempt</h3><div class="v100-attempt">${escapeHTML(attempt)}</div>`:''}<p>Compare the reasoning, not just the words. Did you reach the correct idea?</p><div class="v98-actions"><button class="v100-secondary" data-grade="again">I need another try</button><button data-grade="good">${independent?'I got it on my own':'I understand with help'}</button></div>`;
 box.querySelectorAll('[data-grade]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>grade(c,b.dataset.grade,independent));
 box.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'instant'});
}
function grade(c,grade,independent){
 const state=load(),prev=state.cards[c.id]||{},good=grade==='good',mastered=good&&independent;
 const prior=Number(prev.interval)||0;
 const interval=mastered?(prior>=7?14:prior>=3?7:prior>=1?3:1):good?1:0;
 state.cards[c.id]={interval,success:(Number(prev.success)||0)+(mastered?1:0),assisted:(Number(prev.assisted)||0)+(good&&!independent?1:0),last:Date.now(),next:Date.now()+interval*DAY,needsWork:!good,independent:mastered};
 save(state);sessionAttempts++;repeatCount[c.id]=(repeatCount[c.id]||0)+1;
 if(!good&&repeatCount[c.id]<2)deck.push(c);
 index++;render();
}
function setTheme(v){
 if(window.studyTableTheme){window.studyTableTheme.set(v);return}
 document.documentElement.dataset.studyTheme=v;
 try{localStorage.setItem(THEME,v)}catch{}
 const b=el('v100Theme');if(b){b.textContent=v==='dark'?'Light mode':'Dark mode';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Switch to '+(v==='dark'?'light':'dark')+' mode')}
}
function init(){
 if(el('v98Dialog'))return;
 const d=document.createElement('dialog');d.id='v98Dialog';d.setAttribute('aria-label','Recall practice');
 d.innerHTML='<div class="v100-head"><strong>Study Table · Practice</strong><button type="button" id="v98Close" aria-label="Close practice">Close</button></div><div id="v98Body"></div>';
 document.body.append(d);el('v98Close').onclick=()=>d.close();
 const tools=document.createElement('div');tools.id='v100Tools';
 tools.innerHTML='<button id="v100Theme" type="button">Dark mode</button><button id="v98Launcher" type="button">Practice without hints</button>';
 document.body.append(tools);
 el('v98Launcher').onclick=()=>{d.showModal();menu()};
 el('v100Theme').onclick=()=>setTheme(document.documentElement.dataset.studyTheme==='dark'?'light':'dark');
 let theme='light';try{theme=localStorage.getItem(THEME)||((window.matchMedia&&matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches)?'dark':'light')}catch{}
 setTheme(theme==='dark'?'dark':'light');
 document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
  if(event.target?.id==='v97HomeStart'||event.target?.closest?.('#v97HomeStart')){
   setTimeout(()=>{const study=el('v97Study');if(study&&!el('v98LessonLaunch')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v98LessonLaunch';b.type='button';b.textContent='Recall practice';b.onclick=()=>{study.close();d.showModal();menu()};study.querySelector('.v97-top')?.append(b)}},0);
  }
 });
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
window.studyTableV100={start,menu,getProgress:load,cards:CARDS.length,version:100};
})();
</script>

const KEY='studytable:v102:mastery',DAY=86400000,$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=v=>String(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const names={spanish:'Spanish',piano:'Piano',chess:'Chess',calculus:'Calculus',physics:'Physics',medicine:'Medicine',history:'History',economics:'Economics',business:'Business',life:'Life skills',books:'Books & reading'};
const valid=v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v);
function read(){try{const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'null');return valid(raw)&&valid(raw.progress)?raw:{schema:1,progress:{}}}catch{return {schema:1,progress:{}}}}
function save(s){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(s));return true}catch(e){console.warn('Mastery progress could not be saved',e);return false}}
let currentQuestion=null;let subject='all',queue=[],pos=0,attempt=0,helped=false,solved=false,selected=null,stats={first:0,assisted:0,incorrect:0,worked:0},promptAnswer='',isRetry=false;
function shuffle(arr){const a=arr.slice();for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]]}return a}
function open(sub='all'){
 if(!names[sub]&&sub!=='all')sub='all';subject=sub;
 const d=$('v103skillMastery');if(!d.open)d.showModal();menu();
}
function menu(){const progress=read().progress,by={};ITEMS.forEach(q=>(by[q.subject]??=[]).push(q));
 $('v103skillMain').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-eyebrow">THE PRACTICE STUDIO</div><h2>Choose some practice</h2><p>Choose a subject or mix a few. The next set starts with questions due for review and topics that need more practice.</p><div class="v103skill-badges"><span>66 topics · 46 extra number problems</span><span>3 difficulty levels</span><span>Up to six questions</span></div><div class="v103skill-topics"><button data-subject="all" class="${subject==='all'?'v103skill-selected':''}">Mixed practice<small>Bring everything together</small></button>${Object.entries(by).map(([k,v])=>{const done=v.filter(q=>(progress[q.id]?.independent||0)>=2).length;return `<button data-subject="${k}" class="${subject===k?'v103skill-selected':''}">${names[k]}<small>${done}/${v.length} answered unaided twice</small></button>`}).join('')}</div><details id="v104Notebook"><summary>Practice history and notes</summary><p>Counts below are automatically checked challenge results. Lesson reflections remain your own self-checks.</p><div class="v104-map">${Object.entries(by).map(([key,list])=>{const due=list.filter(q=>progress[q.id]&&progress[q.id].next<=Date.now()).length,seen=list.filter(q=>progress[q.id]?.attempts).length,repairs=list.filter(q=>progress[q.id]?.lastMistake).length;return `<article><strong>${names[key]}</strong><p>${seen}/${list.length} tried · ${due} due · ${repairs} to repair</p><button data-v104-focus="${key}">Practice ${names[key]}</button></article>`}).join('')}</div>${ITEMS.filter(q=>progress[q.id]?.lastError||progress[q.id]?.reflection).map(q=>`<article class="v104-note"><strong>${esc(q.title)}</strong>${progress[q.id].lastError?`<p>Last misconception: ${esc(progress[q.id].lastError)}</p>`:''}${progress[q.id].reflection?`<p>Your note: ${esc(progress[q.id].reflection)}</p>`:''}</article>`).join('')||'<p>Specific feedback and your reflections will collect here as you practice.</p>'}</details><div class="v103skill-actions"><button class="v103skill-primary" id="v103skillStart">Start a practice session →</button><button id="v103skillReview">Review earlier mistakes</button></div><p class="v103skill-caveat">Correct answers are checked automatically for multiple-choice and numeric problems; written explanations are your own reflection. Expertise requires much more practice than this set alone.</p>`;
 $('v103skillMain').querySelectorAll('[data-subject]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{subject=b.dataset.subject;menu()});
 $('v103skillMain').querySelectorAll('[data-v104-focus]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{subject=b.dataset.v104Focus;menu()});$('v103skillStart').onclick=()=>begin(false);$('v103skillReview').onclick=()=>begin(true);
}
function begin(review){
 const data=read().progress;const pool=ITEMS.filter(x=>subject==='all'||x.subject===subject);const candidates=review?pool.filter(x=>{let p=data[x.id];return p&&(p.lastMistake||p.next<=Date.now())}):pool.filter(x=>!data[x.id]||data[x.id].next<=Date.now()||data[x.id].lastMistake);
 if(!candidates.length){$('v103skillMain').innerHTML='<h2>You have caught up on scheduled challenges.</h2><p>Give your memory time to consolidate, or choose optional extra practice now.</p><div class="v103skill-actions"><button id="v103skillFreePractice" class="v103skill-primary">Practice again anyway</button><button id="v103skillReturn">Choose subjects</button></div>';$('v103skillFreePractice').onclick=()=>{queue=shuffle(pool).slice(0,6);pos=0;stats={first:0,assisted:0,incorrect:0,worked:0};question()};$('v103skillReturn').onclick=menu;return}
 // Repair due misconceptions first, then overdue work, then new foundations.
 queue=shuffle(candidates).sort((a,b)=>{const priority=q=>{const p=data[q.id];return p?.lastMistake?0:p&&p.next<=Date.now()?1:2};return priority(a)-priority(b)||a.level-b.level}).slice(0,6);
 pos=0;stats={first:0,assisted:0,incorrect:0,worked:0};question();
}
function question(){const base=queue[pos];if(!base)return finish();const q=variant(base,(Number(read().progress[base.id]?.attempts)||0)%3);currentQuestion=q;attempt=0;helped=false;solved=false;selected=null;promptAnswer='';isRetry=false;
 const steps=['Foundations','Applications','Challenge'];
 $('v103skillMain').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-eyebrow">${esc(names[q.subject])} · ${steps[q.level-1]} · ${pos+1} of ${queue.length}</div><div class="v103skill-progress" aria-label="Session progress"><span style="width:${Math.round(100*pos/queue.length)}%"></span></div><h2>${esc(q.title)}</h2><div class="v103skill-scene">${esc(q.scene)}</div><div class="v103skill-card"><p class="v103skill-explain">${esc(q.ask)}</p><div id="v103skillAnswer"></div><div id="v103skillFeedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div><div class="v103skill-actions"><button id="v103skillHint">Give me a useful hint</button><button id="v103skillCheck" class="v103skill-primary">Check my answer</button><button id="v103skillStuck">Show me the worked answer</button></div><div id="v103skillWorked" hidden></div></div><p class="v103skill-meta">Attempt the problem before asking for help. There is no timer and no penalty for trying.</p>`;
 if(q.type==='choice')$('v103skillAnswer').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-options" role="group" aria-label="Answer options">${shuffle(q.options.map((x,i)=>({x,i}))).map(({x,i},position)=>`<button type="button" data-ans="${i}" aria-pressed="false">${String.fromCharCode(65+position)}. ${esc(x)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
 else $('v103skillAnswer').innerHTML=`<label for="v103skillNum">Your numerical answer${q.unit?' ('+esc(q.unit)+')':''}</label><div><input id="v103skillNum" class="v103skill-num" type="text" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Enter a number"></div>`;
 $('v103skillAnswer').querySelectorAll('[data-ans]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{selected=Number(b.dataset.ans);$('v103skillAnswer').querySelectorAll('[data-ans]').forEach(x=>{x.classList.toggle('v103skill-selected',x===b);x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(x===b))})});
 $('v103skillHint').onclick=()=>{helped=true;$('v103skillFeedback').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-feedback">${esc(q.hint)}</div>`};
 $('v103skillCheck').onclick=()=>check(q);
 $('v103skillStuck').onclick=()=>{helped=true;solution(q,false,true)};
 if(q.type==='number'){$('v103skillNum').focus();$('v103skillNum').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')check(q)})}else $('v103skillAnswer').querySelector('button')?.focus();
}
function check(q){if(solved)return;let correct=false,wrong='';
 if(q.type==='choice'){
  if(selected===null){$('v103skillFeedback').innerHTML='<div class="v103skill-feedback">Choose an answer first, or ask for a hint.</div>';return}
  correct=selected===q.correct;wrong=q.errors[selected]||'Review the key distinction and try again.';
 }else{
  const raw=$('v103skillNum').value.trim();if(!raw){$('v103skillFeedback').innerHTML='<div class="v103skill-feedback">Enter a number first, or ask for a hint.</div>';return}
  if(!/^[+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)(?:e[+-]?\d+)?$/i.test(raw)&&! /^[+-]?\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){$('v103skillFeedback').textContent='Use a number with a decimal point, or correctly grouped thousands.';return}const n=Number(raw.replace(/,/g,''));if(!Number.isFinite(n)){$('v103skillFeedback').innerHTML='<div class="v103skill-feedback">Use a numerical value, such as 3.5 or -2.</div>';return}
  correct=Math.abs(n-q.answer)<=q.tolerance;wrong=q.errors[String(n)]||'Check the formula, units, and intermediate calculation. You can try again or see the full reasoning.';
 }
 attempt++;
 if(correct){if(!helped&&attempt===1)stats.first++;else stats.assisted++;solution(q,true,false);return}
 stats.incorrect++;const missState=read(),missPrev=missState.progress[q.id]||{};missState.progress[q.id]={...missPrev,wrongAttempts:(Number(missPrev.wrongAttempts)||0)+1,lastError:wrong,lastWrongAt:Date.now(),lastMistake:true};save(missState);
 const tag=`${wrong}`;
 if(attempt<2){$('v103skillFeedback').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-feedback">${esc(tag)}<br>You can try again or open the worked answer.</div>`;if(q.type==='choice')$('v103skillAnswer').querySelector(`[data-ans="${selected}"]`)?.classList.add('v103skill-wrong');return}
 helped=true;solution(q,false,false,tag);
}
function solution(q,correct,stuck,note=''){
 if(solved)return;solved=true;if(!correct)stats.worked++;
 $('v103skillHint').disabled=true;$('v103skillCheck').disabled=true;$('v103skillStuck').disabled=true;
 if(q.type==='choice')$('v103skillAnswer').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);else $('v103skillNum').readOnly=true;
 const firstTry=correct&&!helped&&attempt===1;
 const state=read(),prev=state.progress[q.id]||{};const trulyIndependent=firstTry&&(!prev.last||Date.now()-prev.last>=DAY);
 // A miss or hint does not count as unassisted mastery. Review intervals: 1, 3, 7, 14 days.
 if(!firstTry){queue=queue.filter((entry,i)=>i<=pos||entry.subject!==q.subject||entry.level<=q.level)}
 const times=(Number(prev.independent)||0)+(trulyIndependent?1:0);
 const interval=trulyIndependent?(times>=4?14:times===3?7:times===2?3:1):0;
 state.progress[q.id]={...prev,variant:q.variant||0,independent:times,attempts:(Number(prev.attempts)||0)+1,assisted:(Number(prev.assisted)||0)+(trulyIndependent?0:1),next:Date.now()+(trulyIndependent?interval*DAY:correct?DAY:10*60000),last:Date.now(),lastMistake:!correct||helped||attempt>1,reflection:typeof prev.reflection==='string'?prev.reflection:''};save(state);
 const answer=q.type==='choice'?q.options[q.correct]:`${q.answer}${q.unit?' '+q.unit:''}`;
 $('v103skillFeedback').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-feedback">${note?esc(note):correct?'Correct. Here are the steps.':stuck?'Here is the worked answer.':'Compare your steps with these.'}</div>`;
 $('v103skillWorked').hidden=false;
 $('v103skillWorked').innerHTML=`<section class="v103skill-solution"><h3>${correct?'Why your answer works':'Work through this solution'}</h3><p><strong>Answer: ${esc(answer)}</strong></p><p>${esc(q.why)}</p>${!firstTry?'<p>Your remaining session focuses on this level. Repair the method before adding harder questions.</p>':''}<details open><summary>Apply it somewhere new</summary><p>${esc(q.transfer)}</p></details><button id="v104StudyLesson" type="button">Explore ${esc(names[q.subject])} lessons</button><label for="v103skillExplain">Put the idea in your own words (optional)</label><textarea id="v103skillExplain" placeholder="What mattered here? What would you do in a similar problem?"></textarea><p class="v103skill-caveat">This written explanation is a reflection; it is not automatically graded.</p></section><div class="v103skill-actions"><button class="v103skill-primary" id="v103skillNext">${pos+1<queue.length?'Next challenge →':'Finish session →'}</button><button id="v103skillBackToMenu">Choose another subject</button></div>`;
 $('v104StudyLesson').onclick=()=>{$('v103skillMastery').close();if(typeof v35Close==='function')v35Close();window.studyTableV97.open(q.subject)};$('v103skillExplain').value=prev.reflection||'';const saveReflection=()=>{const reflection=$('v103skillExplain')?.value.trim().slice(0,1500);if(typeof reflection==='string'){const current=read();if(current.progress[q.id]){current.progress[q.id].reflection=reflection;save(current)}}};$('v103skillNext').onclick=()=>{saveReflection();pos++;question()};$('v103skillBackToMenu').onclick=()=>{saveReflection();menu()};
 $('v103skillNext').focus();
}
function finish(){const state=read().progress,done=ITEMS.filter(x=>(state[x.id]?.independent||0)>=2).length;
 $('v103skillMain').innerHTML=`<div class="v103skill-eyebrow">SESSION REVIEW</div><h2>Session results</h2><p>You worked through ${queue.length} challenges, with ${stats.first} correct on the first try without help, ${stats.assisted} solved with support, and ${stats.incorrect} incorrect attempts, and ${stats.worked} worked answers studied. The questions you missed will return for review.</p><div class="v103skill-badges"><span>${done}/66 answered unaided twice</span><span>Review when due</span></div><p>For deep proficiency, return to the full lessons, real-world exercises, and interactive labs. Revisit weak areas rather than rushing through the next batch.</p><div class="v103skill-actions"><button class="v103skill-primary" id="v103skillAgain">Practice another set</button><button id="v103skillReturnLessons">Back to learning</button></div>`;
 $('v103skillAgain').onclick=menu;$('v103skillReturnLessons').onclick=()=>$('v103skillMastery').close();
}
// Two authored parameter sets per numeric concept; original questions remain the first exposure.
function variant(q,index){
 if(q.type!=='number'||index===0)return {...q};
 const second=index===2;let scene,ask,answer,why,hint;
 switch(q.id){
 case 'piano-4':{const bpm=second?100:80;scene=`The metronome is set to ${bpm} quarter-note beats per minute.`;ask='How many seconds does one beat last?';answer=60/bpm;why=`60 ÷ ${bpm} = ${answer} seconds per beat.`;hint='A minute has 60 seconds. Divide by beats per minute.';break}
 case 'calculus-1':{const x=second?3:1;scene='Daily output follows f(x)=x³−4x²+2x.';ask=`What is f′(${x})?`;answer=3*x*x-8*x+2;why=`f′(x)=3x²−8x+2. At x=${x}, the result is ${answer}.`;hint='Differentiate each term before substituting.';break}
 case 'calculus-2':{const a=second?3:4,t=second?2:3;scene=`Position is s(t)=${a}t²+2t metres.`;ask=`What is velocity at t=${t} seconds?`;answer=2*a*t+2;why=`v(t)=${2*a}t+2, so v(${t})=${answer} m/s.`;hint='Velocity is the derivative of position.';break}
 case 'calculus-3':{const a=second?2:3,b=second?3:1,x=1;scene=`Let f(x)=(${a}x+${b})³.`;ask='Find f′(1).';answer=3*a*(a*x+b)**2;why=`The chain rule gives 3(${a}x+${b})²×${a}; at x=1 this is ${answer}.`;hint='Multiply the outer derivative by the inner derivative.';break}
 case 'calculus-4':{const a=second?4:3,t=second?2:4;scene=`Water flows into a tank at r(t)=${a}t liters/minute, from t=0 to t=${t} minutes.`;ask='How many liters arrive?';answer=a*t*t/2;why=`Integrate ${a}t from 0 to ${t}: ½×${a}×${t}²=${answer} liters.`;hint='The area under the rate graph gives the amount.';break}
 case 'calculus-5':{const a=second?60:30;scene=`Profit is P(q)=${a}q−q² dollars for continuous q.`;ask='At what q is profit maximized?';answer=a/2;why=`P′=${a}−2q=0 gives q=${answer}. P″=−2 confirms a maximum.`;hint='Find a stationary point, then check the curvature.';break}
 case 'calculus-6':{const x=second?2:0;scene='Let f(x)=ln(x²+1).';ask=`Find f′(${x}).`;answer=2*x/(x*x+1);why=`f′=2x/(x²+1), giving ${answer} at x=${x}.`;hint='Differentiate ln(u) as u′/u.';break}
 case 'physics-1':{const m=second?3:5,f=second?27:30,r=second?6:10;scene=`A ${m} kg cart is pushed right with ${f} N, opposed by ${r} N friction.`;ask='What is the rightward acceleration?';answer=(f-r)/m;why=`Net force=${f}−${r}=${f-r} N. Divide by ${m} kg: ${answer} m/s² right.`;hint='Combine signed forces before dividing by mass.';break}
 case 'physics-2':{const u=second?4:2,a=second?3:4,t=second?5:3;scene=`Initial velocity is ${u} m/s right, with constant acceleration ${a} m/s² right for ${t} s.`;ask='What is final rightward velocity?';answer=u+a*t;why=`v=u+at=${u}+${a}×${t}=${answer} m/s.`;hint='Add the change in velocity to the initial velocity.';break}
 case 'physics-3':{const m=second?4:3,h=second?2:8;scene=`A ${m} kg object drops ${h} m without energy losses. Use g=10 m/s².`;ask='How much gravitational potential energy becomes kinetic energy?';answer=m*10*h;why=`mgh=${m}×10×${h}=${answer} J.`;hint='Use mass, gravitational acceleration and vertical drop.';break}
 case 'physics-4':{const v=second?24:12,r=second?4:3;scene=`An ideal ${v} V source is connected across a ${r} Ω resistor.`;ask='What current flows?';answer=v/r;why=`I=V/R=${v}/${r}=${answer} A.`;hint='Apply Ohm’s law for this resistor.';break}
 case 'physics-5':{const m=second?3:1,n=second?1:2,v=second?8:9;scene=`A ${m} kg cart moving right at ${v} m/s sticks to a ${n} kg cart at rest. External impulse is negligible.`;ask='What is their shared rightward velocity?';answer=m*v/(m+n);why=`Conserve momentum: (${m}×${v})/(${m}+${n})=${answer} m/s right.`;hint='Use total momentum divided by the combined mass.';break}
 case 'physics-6':{const m=second?2:3,v=second?4:2,r=second?4:2;scene=`A ${m} kg object moves in a circle at ${v} m/s with radius ${r} m.`;ask='What is the magnitude of centripetal force?';answer=m*v*v/r;why=`F=mv²/r=${m}×${v}²/${r}=${answer} N.`;hint='The speed is squared; force points inward.';break}
 case 'medicine-3':{const sensitivity=second?96:80,n=second?200:60;scene=`A test has ${sensitivity}% sensitivity. It is used on ${n} people who truly have the disease.`;ask='How many false negatives are expected on average?';answer=n*(100-sensitivity)/100;why=`The missed fraction is ${100-sensitivity}%; ${n}×${(100-sensitivity)/100}=${answer} false negatives.`;hint='Sensitivity is conditional on disease being present.';break}
 case 'economics-1':{const a=second?900:750,b=second?600:500;scene=`A project pays $${a}; accepting it means forgoing an equal-effort project paying $${b}. Other costs are equal.`;ask='What is the incremental monetary benefit of accepting the higher-paid project?';answer=a-b;why=`Compare alternatives: ${a}−${b}=$${answer}.`;hint='Subtract the best forgone alternative.';break}
 case 'economics-3':{const n=second?8:5,i=second?2:3;scene=`Nominal return is ${n}% and inflation is ${i}%. Use the Fisher approximation.`;ask='What is approximate real return, as a percentage?';answer=n-i;why=`Approximate real return=${n}−${i}=${answer}%.`;hint='For the requested approximation, subtract inflation.';break}
 case 'economics-5':{const shirts=second?20:12,bags=second?5:4;scene=`A worker can make ${shirts} shirts or ${bags} bags per day, with a linear production trade-off.`;ask='How many shirts are forgone per extra bag?';answer=shirts/bags;why=`Opportunity cost=${shirts}/${bags}=${answer} shirts per bag.`;hint='Express the forgone output per unit of the chosen output.';break}
 case 'business-1':{const price=second?25:22,cost=second?9:8,fulfill=second?4:3,fee=2;scene=`A box sells for $${price}; production is $${cost}, fulfillment $${fulfill}, and payment fees $${fee}. Exclude fixed costs.`;ask='What is contribution per box?';answer=price-cost-fulfill-fee;why=`${price}−${cost}−${fulfill}−${fee}=$${answer} before fixed costs.`;hint='Deduct all listed variable costs from price.';break}
 case 'business-2':{const price=second?40:30,cost=second?22:21;scene=`Price is $${price} and total variable cost is $${cost}.`;ask='What is contribution margin percentage?';answer=(price-cost)/price*100;why=`(${price}−${cost})/${price}×100=${answer}%.`;hint='Divide contribution dollars by revenue, not by cost.';break}
 case 'business-3':{const c=second?21:9;scene=`First-order contribution is $${c}, before acquisition. There are no repeat orders or other customer-level costs.`;ask='What is the maximum break-even CAC?';answer=c;why=`CAC can use at most the $${c} contribution before first-order contribution after acquisition becomes negative.`;hint='Set contribution after acquisition equal to zero.';break}
 case 'business-4':{const n=second?2000:1500,p=second?3:6;scene=`There are ${n} qualified visitors; ${p}% convert, with one order per buyer.`;ask='How many orders are expected?';answer=n*p/100;why=`${n}×${p/100}=${answer} orders.`;hint='Convert the percentage to a fraction before multiplying.';break}
 case 'business-5':{const first=second?12:6,p=second?.25:.4,repeat=second?8:15;scene=`First-order contribution is $${first}. A fraction ${p} of customers makes exactly one repeat order contributing $${repeat}. Ignore discounting and further orders.`;ask='What is expected lifetime contribution before acquisition?';answer=first+p*repeat;why=`${first}+${p}×${repeat}=$${answer}. The repeat contribution is probability-weighted.`;hint='Add certain first-order contribution and expected repeat contribution.';break}
 case 'life-3':{const p=second?.4:.2,gain=second?500:800,loss=second?100:50;scene=`A project has a ${p*100}% chance of gaining $${gain} and a ${(1-p)*100}% chance of losing $${loss}. Ignore other effects.`;ask='What is expected monetary value?';answer=p*gain-(1-p)*loss;why=`${p}×${gain}−${1-p}×${loss}=$${answer}. An expectation is not a guaranteed outcome.`;hint='Weight each signed outcome by its probability.';break}
 default:return {...q};
 }
 return {...q,scene,ask,answer,why,hint,errors:{},variant:index};
}

 const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v103skillLauncher';b.type='button';b.textContent='Skill challenges';b.title='Work through problems, not just flashcards';b.onclick=()=>open();document.body.append(b);

 const own=(object,key)=>typeof key==='string'&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(object,key);
 const esc=value=>String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
 const normal=value=>value.normalize('NFC').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[−–]/g,'-').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
 const words={half:.5,zero:0,one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,twelve:12,sixteen:16,'one hundred':100,'sixty four':64};
 function result(kind,message){return {kind,message}}
 function check(unit,index,value){
  if(!own(scenarios,unit)||!Number.isInteger(index)||!scenarios[unit].steps[index]||typeof value!=='string')return null;
  const step=scenarios[unit].steps[index],input=normal(value.slice(0,2000));
  if(!input)return result('empty','Enter an answer for this step, or choose “See the teacher work this step”. Your working notes can stay in your own words.');
  const fits=()=>result('fits','That fits this step. '+step.reasoning);
  if(step.kind==='number'){
   let text=input;if(own(words,text))text=String(words[text]);else if(text.startsWith('minus ')&&own(words,text.slice(6)))text=String(-words[text.slice(6)]);
   let match=/^([+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+))\s*(.*)$/.exec(text),value;
   if(match){
    const suffix=match[2].trim().replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/²/g,'^2');
    if(suffix&&!(step.units||[]).includes(suffix))return result('compare','Check the units as well as the number. '+(step.unitLabel?'This step asks for '+step.unitLabel+'. ':'Enter just the value requested here. ')+step.reasoning);
    value=Number(match[1]);
   }else{
    match=/^(?:√\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)|sqrt\((\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\))$/.exec(text);
    if(match)value=Math.sqrt(Number(match[1]||match[2]));
   }
   if(!Number.isFinite(value))return result('compare','I can check a single number here. Keep your calculation in the working notes, then enter its result. You can also compare with the teacher’s reasoning below.');
   if(Math.abs(value-Number(step.answer))<1e-8)return fits();
   return result('revise',own(step.mistakes,String(value))?step.mistakes[String(value)]:'Compare your result with this account: '+step.reasoning);
  }
  if(step.kind==='note'){
   const pitch=input.replace(/^middle c$/,'c4').replace(/^low c$/,'c3').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
   if(pitch===step.answer)return fits();
   return own(step.mistakes,pitch)?result('revise',step.mistakes[pitch]):result('compare','Use the note name and register, such as C3 or D4. '+step.reasoning);
  }
  if(step.kind==='square'){
   const square=input.replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/[.!]$/,'');
   if(square===step.answer)return fits();
   return result(/^[a-h][1-8]$/.test(square)?'revise':'compare',own(step.mistakes,square)?step.mistakes[square]:'Use a board square such as g1. '+step.reasoning);
  }
  if(step.kind==='phrase'){
   const clean=v=>normal(v).replace(/[¿?¡!.,;:]/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(),phrase=clean(input),accepted=[step.answer,...(step.accepted||[])].map(clean);
   if(accepted.includes(phrase))return fits();
   const plain=v=>v.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');
   if(accepted.some(v=>plain(v)===plain(phrase)))return result('revise',step.spellingHelp||'Keep the spelling and accents shown here: '+step.answer);
   for(const [attempt,message] of Object.entries(step.mistakes))if(clean(attempt)===phrase)return result('revise',message);
   return result('compare','Here is one reply that fits this exchange: '+step.answer+' Other wording can work too. Compare what your reply asks or confirms with the complete exchange.');
  }
  const phrase=input.replace(/[¿?¡!.,;:]/g,'').replace(/^quiero\s+/,'').replace(/\s+por favor$/,'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  if(phrase===step.answer)return fits();
  if(phrase.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')===step.answer.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,''))return result('revise','The wording fits this model. Keep the spelling shown here, including accents and ñ: '+step.answer+'.');
  const tokens=phrase.split(' ');
  for(const [token,message] of Object.entries(step.mistakes))if(tokens.includes(token))return result('revise',message);
  return result('compare','I cannot reliably check that wording against this small phrase model. It may express something different. Compare the article, noun and description with: '+step.answer+'. Other ways to make a request can also be valid.');
 }
 function boardMarkup(board){
  const ranks=board.fen.split(' ')[0].split('/');if(ranks.length!==8)return '';
  let cells='',pieces='';const marks=board.marks||[];
  for(let row=0;row<8;row++){
   let file=0;for(const char of ranks[row]){if(/[1-8]/.test(char)){file+=Number(char);continue}const x=32+file*42+21,y=8+row*42+21,white=char===char.toUpperCase();pieces+='<circle cx="'+x+'" cy="'+y+'" r="16" fill="'+(white?'#fff':'#242428')+'" stroke="'+(white?'#242428':'#fff')+'" stroke-width="1.5"/><text x="'+x+'" y="'+(y+8)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="25" font-family="system-ui" fill="'+(white?'#242428':'#fff')+'">'+esc(char.toUpperCase())+'</text>';file++}
   for(let col=0;col<8;col++){const x=32+col*42,y=8+row*42,square='abcdefgh'[col]+(8-row);cells+='<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+y+'" width="42" height="42" fill="'+((row+col)%2?'#bcbcc7':'#f0f0f4')+'"/>'+(marks.includes(square)?'<rect x="'+(x+2)+'" y="'+(y+2)+'" width="38" height="38" fill="none" stroke="#7657bf" stroke-width="3"/>':'')}
  }
  const labels=[...Array(8)].map((_,i)=>'<text x="'+(53+i*42)+'" y="365" text-anchor="middle">'+'abcdefgh'[i]+'</text><text x="18" y="'+(34+i*42)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+(8-i)+'</text>').join('');
  return '<figure class="st132-board"><svg viewBox="0 0 380 377" role="img" aria-labelledby="st132BoardTitle"><title id="st132BoardTitle">'+esc(board.description)+'</title>'+cells+pieces+'<g font-family="system-ui" font-size="16" fill="currentColor">'+labels+'</g></svg><figcaption>'+esc(board.description)+'</figcaption></figure>';
 }
 function referenceMarkup(reference){
  if(!reference)return '';let body=reference.board?boardMarkup(reference.board):'';
  if(reference.table){const t=reference.table;body+='<div class="st132-reference-scroll" role="region" tabindex="0" aria-label="Worked example table"><table><caption>'+esc(t.caption)+'</caption><thead><tr>'+t.headers.map(h=>'<th scope="col">'+esc(h)+'</th>').join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+t.rows.map(row=>'<tr>'+row.map((v,i)=>'<'+(i?'td':'th scope="row"')+((t.languageColumns||[]).includes(i)?' lang="es"':'')+'>'+esc(v)+'</'+(i?'td':'th')+'>').join('')+'</tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}
  return '<details class="st132-coach-reference" open><summary>Follow the example</summary>'+body+'</details>';
 }

 let dialog=null,current=null,part=0,opener=null,recognition=null,voiceGeneration=0;
 const drafts=new Map();
 const el=id=>dialog.querySelector('#'+id);
 const state=()=>{const key=current+':'+part;if(!drafts.has(key))drafts.set(key,{thinking:'',answer:'',help:'',feedback:null});return drafts.get(key)};
 function stopVoice(message=''){
  voiceGeneration++;if(recognition){const old=recognition;recognition=null;try{old.abort()}catch{}}
  if(dialog){el('st128CoachStopVoice').hidden=true;el('st128CoachVoice').disabled=false;if(message)el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent=message}
 }
 function ensure(){
  if(dialog)return;
  dialog=document.createElement('dialog');dialog.id='stepCoach';dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','st128CoachTitle');

 async function startVoice(){
  stopVoice();const generation=voiceGeneration,unit=current,index=part,language=el('st128CoachVoiceLanguage').value;
  const Engine=window.SpeechRecognition||window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if(!Engine){el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent='Local dictation is unavailable in this browser. You can type your thinking instead.';return}
  el('st128CoachVoice').disabled=true;el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent='Checking whether local dictation is ready…';
  try{
   const instance=new Engine();
   if(!('processLocally' in instance)||typeof Engine.available!=='function')throw Error('local-unavailable');
   const ready=await Engine.available({langs:[language],processLocally:true});
   if(generation!==voiceGeneration||!dialog.open||current!==unit||part!==index)return;
   if(ready!=='available')throw Error('local-unavailable');
   instance.processLocally=true;instance.lang=language;instance.continuous=false;instance.interimResults=false;recognition=instance;
   instance.onresult=event=>{
    if(generation!==voiceGeneration||!dialog.open||current!==unit||part!==index)return;
    const text=Array.from(event.results).slice(event.resultIndex||0).filter(r=>r.isFinal).map(r=>r[0].transcript).join(' ');
    if(text){const draft=state();draft.thinking=(draft.thinking+(draft.thinking?'\n':'')+text).slice(0,2000);el('st128CoachThinking').value=draft.thinking;el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent='Added to your working notes. Enter your answer separately when you want to check it.'}
   };
   instance.onerror=()=>{if(generation===voiceGeneration)stopVoice('Dictation could not continue. Your notes are kept; you can type instead.')};
   instance.onend=()=>{if(generation===voiceGeneration){recognition=null;el('st128CoachVoice').disabled=false;el('st128CoachStopVoice').hidden=true;if(el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent.startsWith('Listening'))el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent='Microphone stopped.    el('st128CoachStopVoice').hidden=false;el('st128CoachVoiceStatus').textContent='Listening locally. Say your thinking, then stop the microphone.';instance.start();
  }catch{if(generation===voiceGeneration)stopVoice('Local dictation is not ready in this browser. You can type your thinking instead. No cloud dictation was started.')}
 }

 function validTree(v,depth=0,budget={n:0}){
  if(depth>16||++budget.n>100000)throw Error('The file contains too much nested data.');
  if(v===null||typeof v==='boolean')return;
  if(typeof v==='number'){if(!Number.isFinite(v))throw Error('A saved number is invalid.');return}
  if(typeof v==='string'){if(v.length>MAX)throw Error('A saved text field is too large.');return}
  if(Array.isArray(v)){if(v.length>10000)throw Error('A saved list is too long.');for(const x of v)validTree(x,depth+1,budget);return}

 }
 function pathOK(path,empty=false){
  if(typeof path!=='string'||path.length>1000)return false;if(empty&&path==='')return true;

 }
 function unitOK(c,id){return typeof id==='string'&&(c.unitIds.includes(id)||id===c.projectId||id===c.workshopId||id==='project'||id==='workshop'&&!!c.workshopId||own(c.aliases||{},id))}
 function kind(key){
  if(key==='studytable:v207:chapter-checks')return {part:'chapter-checks'};
  for(const c of courseList())for(const part of ['progress','reading','project-part','workshop-part'])if(key===courseKey(c.subject,part))return {part,c};
  if(key==='studytable:full-course:last')return {part:'course-last'};
  if(key==='studytable_v108_reading_place')return {part:'quick-place'};
  if(notebook.includes(key))return {part:key.slice('study-table-v19:'.length),notebook:true};
  if(key.startsWith('study-table-v19:course-store:')&&pathOK(key.slice('study-table-v19:course-store:'.length)))return {part:'fallback'};
  let m=key.match(/^study-table-v19:v21:finished:(spanish|piano|chess|life|medicine|business|books)$/);if(m)return {part:'guided-finished',subject:m[1]};
  m=key.match(/^study-table-v19:v21:teachback:(spanish|piano|chess|life|medicine|business|books):(0|[1-9]\d*)$/);if(m&&Number(m[2])<window.studyTableGuidedLength(m[1]))return {part:'guided-text'};
  if(key==='study-table-v19:v21:lastGuided')return {part:'guided-place'};
  if(key==='study-table-v19:v21:lastMode')return {part:'guided-mode'};
  if(key==='study-table-v19:v22:remember:v1')return {part:'shell'};
  if(legacyKeys.has(key))return {part:'legacy'};
  throw Error('This file contains a section this edition does not recognize. Use the same or a newer edition.');
 }
 function decode(key,raw){
  if(typeof raw!=='string'||bytes(raw)>MAX)throw Error('A saved section is invalid or too large.');
  const k=kind(key);if(k.part==='reading'){if(!unitOK(k.c,raw))throw Error('A course reading place is not recognized.');return raw}
  if(k.part==='project-part'||k.part==='workshop-part'){if(!/^[0-3]$/.test(raw))throw Error('A saved project or workshop chapter is invalid.');return Number(raw)}
  let v;try{v=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{throw Error('A saved section is not readable JSON.')}
  validTree(v);
  if(k.part==='chapter-checks'){if(!window.StudyTableChapterChecks?.validate(v))throw Error('Chapter-check progress is invalid.');return v;}
  if(k.part==='progress'){
   if(!object(v))throw Error('Course review marks must be an object.');
   for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(v)){if(!k.c.unitIds.includes(id)||!object(r)||Object.keys(r).some(p=>!['seen','recall'].includes(p))||own(r,'seen')&&typeof r.seen!=='boolean'||own(r,'recall')&&!['','ok','review'].includes(r.recall))throw Error('A course review mark is invalid or belongs to a newer unit.')}
  }else if(k.part==='course-last'){
   const c=object(v)&&course(v.subject);if(!c||!unitOK(c,v.unit)||Object.keys(v).some(p=>!['subject','unit','title'].includes(p))||own(v,'title')&&(typeof v.title!=='string'||v.title.length>300))throw Error('The saved course resume place is invalid.');
  }else if(k.part==='quick-place'){
   if(!object(v)||!QUICK.has(v.id)||!quick.some(q=>q.id===v.id&&q.subject===v.subject)||own(v,'mode')&&v.mode!=='quick')throw Error('The saved short-lesson place is invalid.');
  }else if(k.notebook){
   if(k.part==='last'){if(!pathOK(v,true))throw Error('The saved notebook place is invalid.')}
   else if(!Array.isArray(v)||v.length>(k.part==='notes'?5000:1000))throw Error('The notebook list is invalid or too long.');
   else if(k.part==='notes')for(const n of v){if(!object(n)||typeof n.text!=='string'||n.text.length>50000||!pathOK(n.path,true)||typeof n.subject!=='string'||n.subject.length>200||!['number','string'].includes(typeof n.when)||!Number.isFinite(new Date(n.when).getTime()))throw Error('A notebook note is invalid.')}
   else if(v.some(p=>!pathOK(p)))throw Error('A saved notebook link is invalid.');
  }else if(k.part==='fallback'){
   if(!object(v))throw Error('An offline course record is invalid.');
   for(const [childKey,value] of Object.entries(v)){
    if(typeof value!=='string'||value.length>2000000)throw Error('An offline course value is invalid.');
    if(courseList().some(c=>['progress','reading','project-part','workshop-part'].some(p=>courseKey(c.subject,p)===childKey)))decode(childKey,value);
    else {let parsed=value;try{parsed=JSON.parse(value)}catch{}legacy.validate(parsed)}
   }
  }else if(k.part==='guided-finished'){
   if(!Array.isArray(v)||v.some(i=>!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>=window.studyTableGuidedLength(k.subject)))throw Error('An earlier guided-course place is invalid.');
  }else if(k.part==='guided-text'){
   if(typeof v!=='string'||v.length>10000)throw Error('An earlier teach-back note is invalid.');
  }else if(k.part==='guided-place'){
   if(!object(v)||!Number.isInteger(v.index)||v.index<0||v.index>=window.studyTableGuidedLength(v.subject))throw Error('An earlier guided resume place is invalid.');
  }else if(k.part==='guided-mode'){
   if(!['guided','original'].includes(v))throw Error('An earlier reading mode is invalid.');
  }else if(k.part==='shell'){legacy.validate(v);
  }else if(k.part==='legacy'){
   if(!object(v))throw Error('An earlier progress section must be an object.');if(key!=='studytable_v102_learning')legacy.validate(v);
   if(key==='studytable_v102_learning'){
    if(v.schema!==1||!object(v.records))throw Error('The learning record format is invalid.');
    for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(v.records)){
     if(!QUICK.has(id)||!object(r))throw Error('A learning record belongs to an unknown lesson.');
     for(const p of ['attempts','independent','assisted','last','next','interval'])if(own(r,p)&&(!Number.isFinite(r[p])||r[p]<0))throw Error('A learning record has an invalid count or date.');
     for(const p of ['selfReported','lastIndependent','needsWork'])if(own(r,p)&&typeof r[p]!=='boolean')throw Error('A saved learning flag is invalid.');
     if(own(r,'confidence')&&!['unsure','partly','sure'].includes(r.confidence))throw Error('A saved confidence choice is invalid.');
     for(const p of ['predictionDraft','transferDraft','repair'])if(own(r,p)&&(typeof r[p]!=='string'||r[p].length>12000))throw Error('A saved learning draft is invalid.');
    }
   }
  }
  return v;
 }
 function merge(a,b,fileWins=false){
  if(object(a)&&object(b)){const out=Object.assign(Object.create(null),a);for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(b))out[k]=own(a,k)?merge(a[k],v,fileWins):v;return out}
  if(Array.isArray(a)&&Array.isArray(b)){const out=a.slice(),seen=new Set(out.map(canon));for(const x of b){const id=canon(x);if(!seen.has(id)){seen.add(id);out.push(x)}}return out}
  return fileWins?b:a;
 }
 function combine(key,a,b,fileWins){
  const k=kind(key);
  if(k.part==='chapter-checks'){const chapters={...a.chapters};for(const [id,row] of Object.entries(b.chapters))if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(chapters,id)||fileWins)chapters[id]=row;return {version:1,chapters};}
  if(k.part==='reading'||k.part==='project-part'||k.part==='workshop-part'||k.part==='course-last'||k.part==='quick-place'||k.part==='guided-place')return fileWins?b:a;
  if(key==='studytable_v102_learning'){
   const records=Object.assign(Object.create(null),a.records);for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(b.records))if(!own(records,id)||fileWins)records[id]=r;
   return {...a,schema:1,records}; // A whole attempt record wins; counters are never added.
  }
  const value=merge(a,b,fileWins);
  if(k.part==='progress')for(const id of Object.keys(value))if(a[id]?.seen||b[id]?.seen)value[id]={...value[id],seen:true};
  return value;
 }
 const rawValue=(key,value)=>kind(key).part==='reading'?value:JSON.stringify(value);
 function expand(records){
  const out=Object.assign(Object.create(null),records);
  for(const c of courseList()){
   const fk='study-table-v19:course-store:'+c.path;
   let fallback=own(out,fk)?decode(fk,out[fk]):Object.create(null),changed=false;
   for(const p of ['progress','reading','project-part','workshop-part']){
    const key=courseKey(c.subject,p);
    if(!own(out,key)&&own(fallback,key)){out[key]=fallback[key];changed=true}
    if(own(out,key)){if(p==='progress'&&own(fallback,key))out[key]=rawValue(key,combine(key,decode(key,out[key]),decode(key,fallback[key]),false));fallback={...fallback,[key]:out[key]};changed=true}
   }
   if(changed)out[fk]=JSON.stringify(fallback);
  }
  return out;
 }
 function keys(){
  const set=new Set([...legacyKeys,...notebook,'studytable:v207:chapter-checks','studytable:full-course:last','studytable_v108_reading_place']);
  for(const c of courseList())for(const p of ['progress','reading','project-part','workshop-part'])set.add(courseKey(c.subject,p));
  for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){const key=localStorage.key(i);if(key&&(key.startsWith('study-table-v19:course-store:')||key.startsWith('study-table-v19:v21:')||key==='study-table-v19:v22:remember:v1')){try{kind(key);set.add(key)}catch{}}}
  return set;
 }
 function collect(){
  if(typeof READY!=='undefined'&&!READY)throw Error('Wait for the lessons to finish loading, then try again.');
  const records=Object.create(null);for(const key of keys()){const raw=localStorage.getItem(key);if(raw!==null)records[key]=raw}
  for(const [short,value] of Object.entries(window.studyTableShellMemory?.()||{})){const key='study-table-v19:'+short;if(notebook.includes(key)||short.startsWith('course-store:')||short.startsWith('v21:')||short==='v22:remember:v1'){try{kind(key);records[key]=JSON.stringify(value)}catch{}}}
  // Mirror the seven readers' fallback records so a file can move between storage contexts.
  return {format:'study-table-backup',schemaVersion:1,appVersion:APP_VERSION,exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),records:expand(records)};
 }
 function parse(payload){
  validTree(payload);let records;
  if(object(payload)&&payload.format==='study-table-backup'&&payload.schemaVersion===1&&object(payload.records))records=payload.records;
  else if(object(payload)&&payload.format==='studytable-progress'&&[56,57].includes(payload.version)&&object(payload.records))records=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(payload.records).map(([k,v])=>[k,JSON.stringify(v)]));
  else if(object(payload)&&payload.schema===1&&object(payload.records))records={'studytable_v102_learning':JSON.stringify(payload)};
  else if(object(payload)&&[1,2].includes(payload.version)&&Array.isArray(payload.notes)&&Array.isArray(payload.bookmarks)){records={'study-table-v19:notes':JSON.stringify(payload.notes),'study-table-v19:bookmarks':JSON.stringify(payload.bookmarks)};if(payload.recent)records['study-table-v19:recent']=JSON.stringify(payload.recent);
   if(payload.version===2&&payload.guided){const g=payload.guided;if(g.progress)for(const [subject,v] of Object.entries(g.progress))records['study-table-v19:v21:finished:'+subject]=JSON.stringify(v);if(g.teachbacks)for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(g.teachbacks))records['study-table-v19:v21:teachback:'+id]=JSON.stringify(v);if(g.last)records['study-table-v19:v21:lastGuided']=JSON.stringify(g.last);if(g.lastMode)records['study-table-v19:v21:lastMode']=JSON.stringify(g.lastMode)}
  }
  else throw Error('Choose a Study Table progress or notebook backup.');
  if(!Object.keys(records).length)throw Error('This file contains no saved records.');
  for(const [key,raw] of Object.entries(records))decode(key,raw);
  return expand(records);
 }
 function prepare(payload,fileWins=false){
  const currentRecords=collect().records,logicalBefore=Object.create(null);
  const incoming=parse(payload),before=Object.create(null),updates=[],groups={courses:0,notebook:0,earlier:0};let conflicts=0;
  for(const [key,raw] of Object.entries(incoming)){
   const k=kind(key),old=localStorage.getItem(key),effective=own(currentRecords,key)?currentRecords[key]:null;before[key]=old;logicalBefore[key]=effective;const imported=decode(key,raw);let result=imported;
   if(effective!==null){const current=decode(key,effective);if(canon(current)!==canon(imported))conflicts++;result=combine(key,current,imported,fileWins)}
   const value=rawValue(key,result);decode(key,value);
   if(old===null||canon(decode(key,old))!==canon(result)){updates.push([key,value]);groups[k.c||k.part==='course-last'?'courses':k.notebook?'notebook':'earlier']++}
  }
  // Canonical course records also update fallback facades, with the same chosen result.
  const map=Object.fromEntries(updates);
  for(const c of courseList()){
   const changed=['progress','reading','project-part','workshop-part'].map(p=>courseKey(c.subject,p)).filter(k=>own(map,k));if(!changed.length)continue;
   const fk='study-table-v19:course-store:'+c.path,old=localStorage.getItem(fk);if(!own(before,fk))before[fk]=old;
   logicalBefore[fk]=own(currentRecords,fk)?currentRecords[fk]:null;const source=own(map,fk)?decode(fk,map[fk]):logicalBefore[fk]===null?{}:decode(fk,logicalBefore[fk]),value=JSON.stringify({...source,...Object.fromEntries(changed.map(k=>[k,map[k]]))});
   const at=updates.findIndex(([k])=>k===fk);if(at<0)updates.push([fk,value]);else updates[at]=[fk,value];decode(fk,value);
  }
  for(const name of Object.keys(groups))groups[name]=0;for(const [key] of updates){const k=kind(key);groups[k.c||k.part==='course-last'?'courses':k.notebook?'notebook':'earlier']++}const plan=Object.freeze({before:Object.freeze(before),logicalBefore:Object.freeze(logicalBefore),updates:Object.freeze(updates.map(pair=>Object.freeze(pair))),groups:Object.freeze(groups),conflicts,fileWins,records:Object.keys(incoming).length});plans.add(plan);return plan;
 }
 function invalidate(keys){window.studyTableInvalidateShellCache?.(keys)}
 function rollback(attempted,before){
  let ok=true;for(const key of attempted)try{localStorage.removeItem(key)}catch{ok=false}
  for(const key of attempted)if(before[key]!==null)try{localStorage.setItem(key,before[key])}catch{ok=false}
  try{return attempted.every(key=>localStorage.getItem(key)===before[key])}catch{return false}
 }
 function apply(plan){
  if(!plan||!plans.has(plan))throw Error('Preview the file before restoring.');plans.delete(plan);
  for(const [key,raw] of Object.entries(plan.before))if(localStorage.getItem(key)!==raw)throw Error('Saved work changed after the preview. Preview the file again before restoring.');
  const currentRecords=collect().records;for(const [key,raw] of Object.entries(plan.logicalBefore))if((own(currentRecords,key)?currentRecords[key]:null)!==raw)throw Error('Saved work changed after the preview. Preview the file again before restoring.');
  if(!plan.updates.length)return {count:0,message:'This file adds no changes with your selected conflict choice.'};
  recovery=collect();const cachedBefore=window.studyTableShellMemory?.()||{},attempted=[];
  try{for(const [key,raw] of plan.updates){attempted.push(key);localStorage.setItem(key,raw)}for(const [key,raw] of plan.updates)if(localStorage.getItem(key)!==raw)throw Error('Save verification failed.')}
  catch{const ok=rollback(attempted,plan.before);throw Error(ok?'The browser could not save the restore. The previous values were restored.':'The restore could not finish, and some previous values could not be written back. Keep this page open and save the previous-data copy below.')}
  lastUndo={before:Object.fromEntries(plan.updates.map(([key])=>[key,key.startsWith('study-table-v19:')&&own(cachedBefore,key.slice('study-table-v19:'.length))?JSON.stringify(cachedBefore[key.slice('study-table-v19:'.length)]):plan.before[key]])),after:Object.fromEntries(plan.updates)};invalidate(plan.updates.map(([key])=>key));
  return {count:plan.updates.length,message:'Saved the restored work. Reload to refresh open exercises. Undo is available in this page session until you change these records.'};
 }
 function undo(){
  if(!lastUndo)throw Error('There is no restore to undo in this page session.');
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(lastUndo.after))if(localStorage.getItem(key)!==value)throw Error('New work was saved after the restore. Undo will not replace it. Save a backup of your current work first.');
  const currentRecords=collect().records;for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(lastUndo.after))if(!own(currentRecords,key)||canon(decode(key,currentRecords[key]))!==canon(decode(key,value)))throw Error('New work was saved after the restore. Undo will not replace it. Save a backup of your current work first.');
  const attempted=Object.keys(lastUndo.before),after=lastUndo.after;
  if(!rollback(attempted,lastUndo.before)){rollback(attempted,after);throw Error('The browser could not complete Undo. Keep this page open and save the previous-data copy.')}
  lastUndo=null;invalidate(attempted);return 'The values from before this restore are back. Reload to refresh open exercises.';
 }
 function download(payload,name='study-table-progress-v'+APP_VERSION+'.json'){
  const text=JSON.stringify(payload,null,2);if(bytes(text)>MAX)throw Error('This backup exceeds 16 MB.');
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type:'application/json'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.hidden=true;dialog().append(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),30000);
 }
 function status(message){document.getElementById('v124BackupStatus').textContent=message;document.getElementById('v124Undo').disabled=!lastUndo;document.getElementById('v124Recovery').hidden=!recovery}
 function summary(plan){
  const area=document.getElementById('v124BackupPreview');area.replaceChildren();const h=document.createElement('h3');h.textContent='Review this restore';area.append(h);
  const p=document.createElement('p');p.textContent=plan.updates.length+' saved sections would change. '+plan.conflicts+' sections differ from work already in this browser. Nothing has changed yet.';area.append(p);
  const list=document.createElement('ul');for(const [key,label] of [['courses','Full-course places and review marks'],['notebook','Notebook and saved places'],['earlier','Practice records and offline course records']]){const item=document.createElement('li');item.textContent=label+': '+plan.groups[key];list.append(item)}area.append(list);
  document.getElementById('v124Apply').disabled=!plan.updates.length;area.hidden=false;
 }
 async function readFile(file){
  const generation=++readingFile;pending=null;document.getElementById('v124Apply').disabled=true;document.getElementById('v124BackupPreview').hidden=true;document.getElementById('v124FileInfo').hidden=true;
  try{if(!file)return;if(file.size>MAX)throw Error('Progress files must be smaller than 16 MB.');const text=await file.text();if(generation!==readingFile)return;const payload=JSON.parse(text);const plan=prepare(payload,document.getElementById('v124Conflict').value==='file');pending={payload,plan};const info=document.getElementById('v124FileInfo'),date=typeof payload.exportedAt==='string'?new Date(payload.exportedAt):null;info.textContent=file.name.slice(0,300)+(date&&Number.isFinite(date.getTime())?' • saved '+date.toLocaleString():'');info.hidden=false;summary(plan);status('Check the preview and conflict choice, then choose Apply restore.')}
  catch(e){if(generation===readingFile)status('Could not preview: '+e.message)}
 }
 function dialog(){

  document.body.append(d);

  d.querySelector('#v124Export').onclick=()=>{try{download(collect());status('Your progress file is ready to save. Keep a copy somewhere you can find on the other device.')}catch(e){status('Could not save a backup: '+e.message)}};
  d.querySelector('#v124File').onchange=e=>{readFile(e.target.files?.[0]);e.target.value=''};
  d.querySelector('#v124Conflict').onchange=()=>{if(!pending)return;try{pending.plan=prepare(pending.payload,d.querySelector('#v124Conflict').value==='file');summary(pending.plan);status('Preview updated. Apply restore to save these changes.')}catch(e){pending=null;d.querySelector('#v124Apply').disabled=true;status('Could not preview: '+e.message)}};
  d.querySelector('#v124Apply').onclick=()=>{try{if(!pending)throw Error('Choose and preview a file first.');const result=apply(pending.plan);pending=null;d.querySelector('#v124Apply').disabled=true;d.querySelector('#v124BackupPreview').hidden=true;status(result.message)}catch(e){pending=null;d.querySelector('#v124Apply').disabled=true;d.querySelector('#v124BackupPreview').hidden=true;status(e.message)}};
  d.querySelector('#v124Undo').onclick=()=>{try{status(undo())}catch(e){status(e.message)}};
  d.querySelector('#v124Recovery').onclick=()=>{try{if(recovery)download(recovery,'study-table-before-restore.json')}catch(e){status(e.message)}};
  d.querySelector('#v124Reload').onclick=()=>location.reload();return d;
 }
 function open(trigger){const d=dialog();returnFocus=trigger instanceof HTMLElement?trigger:document.activeElement;if(!d.open){readingFile++;pending=null;d.querySelector('#v124Apply').disabled=true;d.querySelector('#v124BackupPreview').hidden=true;d.querySelector('#v124FileInfo').hidden=true;d.querySelector('#v124Conflict').value='local';d.showModal()}status(lastUndo?'You can undo the last restore in this page session.':'Choose Save a backup file, or preview a file to restore it.');d.querySelector('#v124BackupClose').focus()}
 function mount(){

</script><script id="modal-access-v119">// Older studio overlays use aria-modal without the browser's native dialog behavior.
// Give those overlays background isolation and keyboard/focus handling. Native
// showModal() dialogs keep the browser's own handling, including nested dialogs.
(function(){
 'use strict';
 const focusable='button:not([disabled]),a[href],input:not([disabled]),select:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),iframe,[tabindex]';
 const ignored=new Set(['SCRIPT','STYLE','LINK','META','TEMPLATE','DIALOG']);
 const originalInert=new Map(),records=new WeakMap();
 let active=null,lastOutside=document.activeElement,working=false,queued=false,tools=null,toolsHome=null;
 const visible=el=>el.isConnected&&el.getClientRects().length>0&&getComputedStyle(el).visibility!=='hidden'&&!el.closest('[hidden]');
 const nativeModal=()=>document.querySelector('dialog:modal');
 const buttons=modal=>[...modal.querySelectorAll(focusable)].filter(el=>el.tabIndex>=0&&visible(el)&&!el.closest('[inert]'));
 function closeButton(modal){return [...modal.querySelectorAll('button')].find(b=>visible(b)&&!b.disabled&&(/^close\b/i.test(b.getAttribute('aria-label')||'')||/^close(?:\s|[×✕]|$)/i.test(b.textContent.trim())||/(?:Close|Exit|close)$/.test(b.id)))}
 function focusInside(modal){const target=closeButton(modal)||buttons(modal)[0]||modal;if(target===modal&&!modal.hasAttribute('tabindex')){modal.tabIndex=-1;records.get(modal).addedTabindex=true}target.focus({preventScroll:true})}
 function release(){for(const [el,wasInert] of originalInert)el.inert=wasInert;originalInert.clear()}
 function placeTools(modal){
  // This floating control is part of the active screen, not its background.
  // Move the existing node so its event handlers and native dialog stay intact.
  tools=tools||document.getElementById('v101ToolsButton');if(!tools)return;
  if(!toolsHome){toolsHome=document.createComment('Learning tools original position');tools.before(toolsHome)}
  if(originalInert.has(tools)){tools.inert=originalInert.get(tools);originalInert.delete(tools)}
  if(modal){if(tools.parentElement!==modal)modal.append(tools)}
  else if(toolsHome.parentNode&&tools.previousSibling!==toolsHome)toolsHome.after(tools);
 }
 function isolate(modal){
  // Isolate siblings along the entire ancestor path, not only body children.
  for(let branch=modal;branch.parentElement&&branch!==document.body;branch=branch.parentElement){
   for(const sibling of branch.parentElement.children){
    if(sibling===branch||!(sibling instanceof HTMLElement)||ignored.has(sibling.tagName))continue;
    if(!originalInert.has(sibling))originalInert.set(sibling,sibling.inert);sibling.inert=true;
   }
  }
 }
 function topOverlay(){
  const candidates=[...document.querySelectorAll('[aria-modal="true"]')].filter(el=>el.tagName!=='DIALOG'&&!el.closest('dialog')&&visible(el));
  const z=el=>{let value=0;for(let node=el;node&&node!==document.body;node=node.parentElement){const n=Number.parseInt(getComputedStyle(node).zIndex,10);if(Number.isFinite(n))value=Math.max(value,n)}return value};
  return candidates.map((el,index)=>({el,index,z:z(el)})).sort((a,b)=>a.z-b.z||a.index-b.index).at(-1)?.el||null;
 }
 function restore(target){if(target instanceof HTMLElement&&visible(target)&&!target.closest('[inert]'))target.focus({preventScroll:true})}
 function sync(){
  if(working)return;working=true;
  try{
   const next=topOverlay();
   if(next!==active){
    const previous=active,closed=previous&&!visible(previous),record=previous&&records.get(previous);
    release();active=next;placeTools(next);
    if(next&&!records.has(next))records.set(next,{returnFocus:previous?.contains(document.activeElement)?document.activeElement:lastOutside});
    if(closed){if(record?.addedTabindex)previous.removeAttribute('tabindex');records.delete(previous)}
    if(next)isolate(next);
    if(!nativeModal()){
     if(closed&&record?.returnFocus&&(!next||next.contains(record.returnFocus)))restore(record.returnFocus);
     if(next&&!next.contains(document.activeElement))focusInside(next);
    }
   }else if(active){
    placeTools(active);
    isolate(active); // Also isolate siblings inserted while the overlay is open.
    if(!nativeModal()&&!active.contains(document.activeElement))focusInside(active);
   }
  }finally{working=false}
 }
 function schedule(){if(queued)return;queued=true;queueMicrotask(()=>{queued=false;sync()})}
 document.addEventListener('focusin',event=>{
  if(working)return;sync();if(nativeModal())return;
  if(active){if(!active.contains(event.target))focusInside(active)}
  else if(event.target instanceof HTMLElement&&!event.target.closest('[aria-modal="true"]'))lastOutside=event.target;
 },true);
 document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
  sync();
  if(nativeModal()){
   // Keep the browser's default Tab/cancel behavior, while preventing old
   // document handlers from also closing or trapping the background studio.
   if(event.key==='Escape'||event.key==='Tab')event.stopImmediatePropagation();
   return;
  }
  if(!active)return;
  if(event.key==='Escape'){
   const close=closeButton(active);if(close){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();close.click();sync()}
  }else if(event.key==='Tab'){
   const list=buttons(active),first=list[0],last=list.at(-1),focused=document.activeElement;
   if(!first){event.preventDefault();focusInside(active)}
   else if(!active.contains(focused)||focused===active||(event.shiftKey&&focused===first)||(!event.shiftKey&&focused===last)){
    event.preventDefault();(event.shiftKey?last:first).focus();
   }
  }
 },true);
 new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(document.body,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden','class','style','open','aria-modal']});
 sync();
})();
</script>

const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const own=(o,k)=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(o,k);
