const subject=()=>{const key=$('st163-subject')?.value;return own(BANK,key)?key:'spanish'};
function reset(key){state={subject:own(BANK,key)?key:'spanish',step:'question',selected:null,transferSelected:null,help:null,note:'',sample:false,initialWrong:null,hingeChoice:null,hingeModel:false,attempts:0,transferRevealed:false};}
function sparkText(){const s=subject(),item=BANK[s];const el=$('st160-spark-question');if(el&&el.textContent!==item.teaser)el.textContent=item.teaser;const name=$('st160-spark-subject');if(name&&name.textContent!==item.name)name.textContent=item.name;}
function mount(){
 const hero=$('v90Hero');if(!hero)return;
 if(!$('st160-spark')){
  const div=document.createElement('section');div.id='st160-spark';div.className='st160-spark';
  div.setAttribute('aria-label','Five-minute curiosity challenge');
  div.innerHTML='<div class="st160-spark-top">A curious five minutes · <span id="st160-spark-subject"></span></div>'+
   '<label class="st163-choose" for="st163-subject">What are you curious about?</label>'+
   '<select id="st163-subject" aria-label="Choose a learning subject">'+allowed.map(key=>'<option value="'+esc(key)+'">'+esc(BANK[key].name)+'</option>').join('')+'</select>'+
   '<h2 id="st160-spark-question"></h2>'+
   '<p>Make a prediction. Get help with the idea you missed. Try it in a new situation, then carry on into the course.</p>'+
   '<div class="st160-spark-actions"><button type="button" class="st160-start" id="st160-open">Explore this question</button><span class="st160-small">No timer. No score. Your progress stays yours.</span></div>';
  const lede=hero.querySelector('.v90-lede');
  if(lede)lede.after(div);else hero.prepend(div);
 }
 sparkText();
}

// Choice-specific coaching is authored. We do not infer an unseen thought process from a click.
const V161_DIAGNOSES={"spanish": {"1": "You answered with a location question. The server is asking where the coffee should be served, not where someone is.", "2": "You made a sentence about yourself rather than choosing one of the two service options. A short phrase is enough."}, "piano": {"1": "You treated beats as a count of new key presses. A held note uses a beat without adding a fresh attack.", "2": "You stopped counting when the key stayed down. The pulse continues even when the pitch does not change."}, "chess": {"0": "You noticed there were no legal moves, but missed the other requirement: checkmate means the king is currently attacked.", "2": "Castling is a particular king-and-rook move, not a name for a position where no move is legal."}, "physics": {"0": "You treated acceleration as though the object begins at its fastest speed. It starts from rest and speeds up.", "1": "You saw that later intervals are longer, but the third second is longer still because speed keeps increasing."}, "calculus": {"0": "You picked the left endpoint. With equal profits at $18 and $20 on this symmetric curve, the peak lies between them.", "2": "You picked the right endpoint. Equal endpoint profits do not make either endpoint the maximum."}, "medicine": {"0": "You used the test’s sensitivity as though it were the probability a positive result is genuine. You also need the base rate and false positives.", "2": "A 50–50 split would be a coincidence, not something test accuracy guarantees. Compare the actual positive counts."}, "business": {"0": "You focused on margin per sale and missed the customers lost after the price rise. Profit depends on both.", "1": "You assumed price changes leave profit unchanged. In this demand model, the drop in volume matters."}, "history": {"0": "A higher rate would require more incidents relative to trips. Here incidents stay at 18 while trips double.", "1": "An unchanged incident COUNT is not an unchanged RATE. The number of trips—the denominator—doubled."}, "economics": {"1": "More buyers chasing fixed supply usually creates stronger competition, not a lower market-clearing price.", "2": "Unchanged production costs do not imply an unchanged price. Buyer demand also shapes the market equilibrium."}, "life": {"0": "Opening a suspicious link is not a safe authenticity check. A convincing fake site can gather passwords and codes.", "2": "Replying with account details exposes information to whoever controls the message, whether or not they are the bank."}, "books": {"0": "A narrator’s confidence does not settle whether their story matches observable events.", "2": "One contradiction is a reason to inspect the text more carefully, not to discard every event in the narrative."}};
function v161Feedback(item){
 if(state.selected===item.answer)return '';
 const message=V161_DIAGNOSES[state.subject]?.[state.selected]||item.trap;
 return '<section class="st161-repair" aria-label="Inspect your answer"><h3>Why that answer is tempting</h3><p>You chose: <strong>'+esc(item.choices[state.selected])+'</strong></p><p>'+esc(message)+'</p><p class="st161-note">This addresses the choice you made, not a claim about how you were thinking.</p></section>';
}

// V162: authored prerequisite checks and contrastive transfer cues. All interaction is local.
const V162_COACH={"spanish": {"hinge": {"q": "The server points to a table and says “¿Para aquí?” What option are they offering?", "choices": ["For here", "Anything else?", "Where are you?"], "answer": 0, "hint": "“Aquí” means “here.” The server is offering a place to enjoy your drink.", "ok": "Right: “aquí” is here; “llevar” is to take away. A normal café reply can be a short phrase.", "model": "“Para aquí” = for here. “Para llevar” = to go. Pick the phrase that matches what you want."}, "transferClues": {"0": "“¿De dónde eres?” means “Where are you from?” It does not answer whether you want anything else.", "2": "“Estoy para llevar” talks about yourself, not whether the order is complete. A note attack occurs only when a new key press begins.", "ok": "Exactly. The counted beat continues while the previous E is still sounding.", "model": "Attack = a key starts sounding. Hold = the same key keeps sounding. Both can occupy beats, but only one adds an attack."}, "transferClues": {"0": "Count all eight beats, including the held note; do not count only new key presses.", "2": "60 BPM is one beat per second—not two seconds per beat."}}, "chess": {"hinge": {"q": "Besides having no legal escape, what condition is required for checkmate?", "choices": ["The king is currently under attack", "Both players have no queens", "The king has moved before"], "answer": 0, "hint": "“Check” is the word for a currently attacked king. It is half the word “checkmate.”", "ok": "Yes. The difference between checkmate and stalemate hinges on whether the king is currently in check.", "model": "No legal moves + in check = checkmate. No legal moves + NOT in check = stalemate."}, "transferClues": {"0": "The new king IS attacked. Stalemate requires a king that is not currently attacked.", "2": "Chess does not let a player pass their turn to escape an otherwise terminal position."}}, "physics": {"hinge": {"q": "An object starts from rest and accelerates steadily. What happens to its speed as each second passes?", "choices": ["It stays unchanged", "It gets smaller", "It keeps increasing"], "answer": 2, "hint": "Acceleration means a change in velocity. Here, it acts in the direction of the fall.", "ok": "Right. Each later second begins at a greater speed than the last.", "model": "At 10 m/s² from rest: the speed at 1 s is 10 m/s, at 2 s is 20, and at 3 s is 30."}, "transferClues": {"0": "5 + 15 + 25 includes all three one-second intervals; it is more than 30.", "2": "60 m would overcount early distance; the object started from rest and did not move at its final speed throughout."}}, "calculus": {"hinge": {"q": "On a curve symmetric around its peak, what is true of two points equally far left and right of the peak?", "choices": ["The left one must be higher", "They have the same height", "The right one must be higher"], "answer": 1, "hint": "Imagine folding a symmetric hill down its vertical center line.", "ok": "Exactly. Equal profit at matching distances can reveal where the center of a symmetric peak lies.", "model": "For a symmetric hill, the heights at center − 1 and center + 1 match, even though the center is higher."}, "transferClues": {"1": "The curve must stop climbing at the exact smooth interior top; the slope there cannot remain positive.", "2": "The curve must stop falling at the exact smooth interior top; the slope there cannot remain negative."}}, "medicine": {"hinge": {"q": "There are 9,900 healthy people. Compare that with the 90 true positives among the 100 affected people.", "model": "5% of 9,900 = 495 false positives. 90% of 100 = 90 true positives. That is why base rates matter."}, "transferClues": {"0": "The base rate rose from 1% to 10%, so the original 15% share does not carry over unchanged.", "2": "90% sensitivity is the share of affected people testing positive, not the share of positive tests that are genuine."}}, "business": {"hinge": {"q": "Price per sale goes up, but units sold go down. Do those facts alone tell you which way total profit moves?", "choices": ["Yes, profit must rise", "Yes, profit must fall", "No, compare the two effects"], "answer": 2, "hint": "Profit depends on how much each sale contributes AND how many sales happen. The two changes pull opposite ways.", "ok": "Exactly. To decide what actually happens, you must compare the change in margin with the change in sales volume.", "model": "Contribution from sales = (price − unit cost) × quantity. const V162_CHOICENOTES={"spanish": {"1": "This asks where someone is. The server instead offers two ways to serve the coffee.", "2": "This describes the speaker, rather than selecting one of the two café options."}, "piano": {"1": "This treats beats as new key presses. A held key can occupy another beat without a new attack.", "2": "The pulse does not stop just because the finger stays on the key."}, "chess": {"0": "No legal moves is not sufficient for checkmate. The king must also be in check.", "2": "Castling is a king-and-rook move, not the outcome of having no legal move."}, "physics": {"0": "The object starts at its slowest speed, not its fastest.", "1": "Speed continues increasing into the third second, so the third interval needs comparison too."}, "calculus": {"0": "The left endpoint has the same profit as the right endpoint; neither is the peak of the specified symmetric curve.", "2": "The right endpoint has the same profit as the left endpoint; neither is the peak of the specified symmetric curve."}, "medicine": {"0": "This answer would fit a much larger starting likelihood, but it is not supported by the test sensitivity alone. False positives and the base rate matter.", "2": "A 50–50 split is not guaranteed by sensitivity and specificity. Compare the actual counts of positive results."}, "business": {"0": "A higher margin on each sale cannot by itself establish higher total profit; the number of sales also changes.", "1": "Unchanged profit does not follow from the higher price, because the demand model also changes the sales volume."}, "history": {"0": "With the same number of incidents and twice as many trips, the rate cannot double.", "1": "An unchanged count is not an unchanged rate when the number of trips doubles."}, "economics": {"1": "More buyers competing for fixed supply generally means more demand, not downward price pressure.", "2": "An unchanged production cost does not imply an unchanged equilibrium price when buyer demand rises."}, "life": {"0": "Opening the message link does not verify authenticity. A fake site can look convincingly official.", "2": "Replying gives account details to whoever controls the message; the display name is not reliable verification."}, "books": {"0": "The narrator’s confidence does not settle whether the words match the described events.", "2": "One contradiction is a reason to investigate closely, not to dismiss every part of the story."}};
function coachRecord(){return V162_COACH[state.subject]}
function repairMarkup(item){
 const rec=coachRecord().hinge,selectedOption=item.choices[state.selected];
 const diagnosis=V162_CHOICENOTES[state.subject]?.[state.selected]||item.trap;
 let html='<p class="st160-kicker">LET’S FIX THE MIX-UP · '+esc(item.name)+'</p><h2 id="st160-current" tabindex="-1">First, one smaller question.</h2>'+
 '<section class="st162-mixup" aria-label="Your earlier choice"><p class="st162-eyebrow">You chose</p><strong>'+esc(selectedOption)+'</strong><p>'+esc(diagnosis)+'</p><small>A choice tells us what you clicked, not what you were thinking.</small></section>'+
 '<p class="st162-prompt">'+esc(rec.q)+'</p><div class="st160-options" role="group" aria-label="One-step prerequisite question">'+
 rec.choices.map((choice,i)=>'<button type="button" data-st162-hinge="'+i+'" aria-pressed="'+(state.hingeChoice===i)+'">'+esc(choice)+'</button>').join('')+'</div>';
 if(state.hingeChoice!==null){
  const right=state.hingeChoice===rec.answer;
  html+='<div class="st162-check '+(right?'st162-check-good':'')+'" role="status"><strong>'+(right?'Yes—that’s the missing piece.':'Not quite. Here is the clue.')+'</strong><p>'+esc(right?rec.ok:rec.hint)+'</p></div>';
 }
 if(state.hingeModel)html+='<div class="st160-support"><p>'+esc(rec.model)+'</p></div>';
 html+='<div class="st160-nav"><button type="button" class="st160-primary" data-st162="rethink">'+(state.hingeChoice===rec.answer?'Revisit the original question →':'Try the original again →')+'</button><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st162="model">'+(state.hingeModel?'Hide':'Show')+' a tiny worked example</button><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st162="reveal">Show me the full explanation</button></div>';
 return html;
}
function transferRepairMarkup(item){
 const clue=coachRecord().transferClues[state.transferSelected]||item.trap;
 return '<p class="st160-kicker">USE THE IDEA · TAKE ANOTHER LOOK</p><h2 id="st160-current" tabindex="-1">A useful distinction.</h2>'+
 '<section class="st162-mixup"><p class="st162-eyebrow">Your choice</p><strong>'+esc(item.transfer.choices[state.transferSelected])+'</strong><p>'+esc(clue)+'</p></section>'+
 '<p class="st160-intro">Try the new situation again, or examine the full worked reasoning. Neither choice affects a score.</p>'+
 '<div class="st160-nav"><button type="button" class="st160-primary" data-st162="retry-transfer">Try the new situation again →</button><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st162="transfer-answer">Show the worked reasoning</button><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st162="back-explain">Review the original explanation</button></div>';
}
function evidence(item){
 let html='<div class="st160-evidence" aria-label="Evidence from the example">'+item.facts.map(([k,v])=>'<div class="st160-fact"><span>'+esc(k)+'</span><b>'+esc(v)+'</b></div>').join('')+'</div>';
 if(item.bars){html+='<div aria-label="Comparison chart">'+item.bars.map(([name,v,max])=>'<div class="st160-bar"><span>'+esc(name)+'</span><span class="st160-track"><span class="st160-fill" style="display:block;width:'+Math.min(100,Math.max(0,100*v/max))+'%"></span></span><b>'+esc(v)+'</b></div>').join('')+'</div>'}
 return html;
}
function render(){
 const d=$('st160-quest'),body=$('st160-body');if(!d||!body)return;
 const item=selected(),phase=state.step;
 const progress={question:0,repair:1,reveal:2,transfer:3,'transfer-repair':3,finished:4}[phase]||0;
 $('st160-quest-step').textContent=['A question','A smaller step','The explanation','A new situation','What you learned'][progress];
 $('st160-quest-progress').innerHTML=[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>'<span class="'+(i<=progress?'active':'')+'" aria-hidden="true"></span>').join('');
 let markup='';
 if(phase==='question'){
  markup='<p class="st160-kicker">START WITH A GUESS · '+esc(item.name)+'</p><h2 id="st160-current" tabindex="-1">'+esc(item.question)+'</h2><p class="st160-intro">Make your best guess. If it misses, we’ll find the missing piece before showing the answer.</p><div class="st160-options" role="group" aria-label="Your prediction">'+item.choices.map((c,i)=>'<button type="button" data-st160-choice="'+i+'">'+esc(c)+'</button>').join('')+'</div><p class="st160-note">You can change course or close whenever you like.</p>';
 } else if(phase==='repair'){
  markup=repairMarkup(item);
 } else if(phase==='reveal'){
  const accurate=state.selected===item.answer;
  markup='<p class="st160-kicker">SEE WHY IT WORKS · SEE WHAT HAPPENED</p><h2 id="st160-current" tabindex="-1">'+esc(accurate?'Here’s why that works.':'Here’s the surprising part.')+'</h2><div class="st160-feedback" role="status"><strong>'+esc(item.choices[item.answer])+'</strong><p>'+esc(item.explanation)+'</p></div>'+v161Feedback(item)+evidence(item)+'<p class="st160-intro">What part would you like to unpack?</p><div class="st160-options" role="group" aria-label="Choose how you need help">'+[['small','Make it simpler'],['why','But why does that happen?'],['trap','Show me the tempting mistake'],['example','Show a tiny example']].map(([k,v])=>'<button type="button" data-st160-help="'+k+'" aria-pressed="'+(state.help===k)+'">'+esc(v)+'</button>').join('')+'</div>'+(state.help?'<div class="st160-support" role="status"><p>'+esc(item[state.help])+'</p></div>':'')+'<div class="st160-nav"><button type="button" class="st160-primary" data-st160="transfer">Try a new situation →</button><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st160="restart">Start over</button></div>';
 } else if(phase==='transfer'){
  markup='<p class="st160-kicker">USE IT SOMEWHERE NEW · USE THE IDEA</p><h2 id="st160-current" tabindex="-1">'+esc(item.transfer.q)+'</h2><p class="st160-intro">This is a different situation. See whether the idea still holds.</p><div class="st160-options" role="group" aria-label="New situation choices">'+item.transfer.choices.map((c,i)=>'<button type="button" data-st160-transfer="'+i+'">'+esc(c)+'</button>').join('')+'</div><div class="st160-nav"><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st160="back">Back to explanation</button></div>';
 } else if(phase==='transfer-repair'){
  markup=transferRepairMarkup(item);
 } else {
  const accurate=state.transferSelected===item.transfer.answer;
  markup='<p class="st160-kicker">PUT IT INTO WORDS · MAKE IT YOURS</p><h2 id="st160-current" tabindex="-1">'+esc(accurate?'You carried the idea across.':'Here’s the worked reasoning.')+'</h2><div class="st160-feedback" role="status"><strong>'+esc(item.transfer.choices[item.transfer.answer])+'</strong><p>'+esc(item.transfer.why)+'</p></div><label class="st160-reason-label" for="st160-reason">In your own words, what makes this work? (Optional)</label><textarea id="st160-reason" maxlength="1600" placeholder="One sentence is enough. Nobody is grading your wording.">'+esc(state.note)+'</textarea><p class="st160-note">Your explanation stays on this page and is not assessed or saved. Compare it against the model when you are ready.</p><button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st160="sample">'+(state.sample?'Hide the example reasoning':'Compare with example reasoning')+'</button>'+(state.sample?'<div class="st160-support"><p>'+esc(item.why)+'</p></div>':'')+'<div class="st160-nav"><button type="button" class="st160-primary" data-st160="lesson">'+(window.studyTableFullCourses?.courses?.some(c=>c.subject===state.subject)?'Continue into a real lesson →':'Explore the subject library →')+'</button>'+(window.studyTableFullCourses?.courses?.some(c=>c.subject===state.subject)?'<button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st160="path">Choose a story, lesson or new problem</button>':'')+(item.experiment&&window.studyTableConnectionsV155?'<button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st160="experiment">Try the interactive experiment</button>':'')+'<button type="button" class="st160-secondary" data-st160="restart">Try this question again</button></div>';
 }
 body.innerHTML=markup;
 body.querySelector('#st160-current')?.focus({preventScroll:true});body.scrollTop=0;
}
function makeDialog(){
 let d=$('st160-quest');if(d)return d;
 d=document.createElement('dialog');d.id='st160-quest';d.setAttribute('aria-label','Five-minute curiosity question');
 d.innerHTML='<div class="st160-frame"><div class="st160-head"><strong><span id="st160-quest-step">A question</span> · Study Table</strong><div class="st160-progress" id="st160-quest-progress" aria-hidden="true"></div><button type="button" class="st160-close" data-st160="close" aria-label="Close curiosity session">Close ✕</button></div><div class="st160-body" id="st160-body"></div></div>';
 document.body.append(d);d.addEventListener('close',()=>{if(d.open)return;if(returnFocus?.isConnected)returnFocus.focus({preventScroll:true})});return d;
}
function open(key,trigger){const dialog=makeDialog();returnFocus=trigger?.isConnected?trigger:document.activeElement;reset(key||subject());render();if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();$('st160-body').querySelector('h2')?.focus({preventScroll:true})}
function close(){const dialog=$('st160-quest');if(dialog?.open)dialog.close()}
function lesson(){const item=selected(),course=window.studyTableFullCourses?.courses?.find(c=>c.subject===state.subject);close();if(course){window.studyTableFullCourses.open(state.subject,item.unit);return}const b=$('v90Subjects');if(b){b.click();b.focus({preventScroll:true});return}document.getElementById('v90Continue')?.focus({preventScroll:true})}
function experiment(){const id=selected().experiment;close();if(id)window.studyTableConnectionsV155?.open(id)}
document.addEventListener('change',event=>{if(event.target?.id==='st163-subject')sparkText()},false);
document.addEventListener('input',event=>{if(event.target?.id==='st160-reason')state.note=event.target.value},false);
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
 const target=event.target.closest?.('button');if(!target)return;
 if(target.id==='st160-open'){open(subject(),target);return}
 if(!target.closest('#st160-quest'))return;
 if(target.hasAttribute('data-st160-choice')){state.selected=Number(target.dataset.st160Choice);state.attempts+=1;state.help=null;if(state.selected===selected().answer){state.step='reveal'}else{if(state.initialWrong===null)state.initialWrong=state.selected;state.hingeChoice=null;state.hingeModel=false;state.step='repair'}render();return}
 if(target.hasAttribute('data-st160-help')){state.help=target.dataset.st160Help;render();$('st160-body').querySelector('[data-st160-help="'+state.help+'"]')?.focus({preventScroll:true});return}
 if(target.hasAttribute('data-st160-transfer')){state.transferSelected=Number(target.dataset.st160Transfer);state.step=state.transferSelected===selected().transfer.answer?'finished':'transfer-repair';state.sample=false;render();return}
 if(target.hasAttribute('data-st162-hinge')){state.hingeChoice=Number(target.dataset.st162Hinge);render();$('st160-body').querySelector('[data-st162-hinge="'+state.hingeChoice+'"]')?.focus({preventScroll:true});return}
 if(target.hasAttribute('data-st162')){
  const action=target.dataset.st162;
  if(action==='rethink'){state.step='question';render()}
  if(action==='model'){state.hingeModel=!state.hingeModel;render();$('st160-body').querySelector('[data-st162="model"]')?.focus({preventScroll:true})}
  if(action==='reveal'){state.step='reveal';render()}
  if(action==='retry-transfer'){state.step='transfer';state.transferSelected=null;render()}
  if(action==='transfer-answer'){state.transferRevealed=true;state.step='finished';render()}
  if(action==='back-explain'){state.step='reveal';render()}
  return;
 }
 const action=target.dataset.st160;
 if(action==='close')close();
 if(action==='transfer'){state.step='transfer';render()}
 if(action==='back'){state.step='reveal';render()}
 if(action==='restart'){reset(state.subject);render()}
 if(action==='retry-transfer'){state.step='transfer';state.transferSelected=null;render()}
 if(action==='sample'){state.sample=!state.sample;const sample=$('st160-body').querySelector('.st160-support');if(state.sample){if(!sample){const section=document.createElement('div');section.className='st160-support';section.textContent=selected().why;target.after(section)}}else sample?.remove();target.textContent=state.sample?'Hide the example reasoning':'Compare with example reasoning'}
 if(action==='lesson')lesson();if(action==='path'){const key=state.subject;close();window.studyTableFullCourses?.open(key)}if(action==='experiment')experiment();
},false);
let observer=null;
function init(){document.title="Study Table — Use What You Learn (V199)";makeDialog();mount();if(!observer){observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{if($('v90Hero'))mount()});observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true})}}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
window.studyTableFocusV163={version:163,open:(key)=>open(key),subjects:allowed,content:BANK,inspect:()=>({...state}),reset:()=>{reset(state.subject);render()},mount};
})();
</script>
